\displaystyle\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2d$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle(2\gamma-d)^{+}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2d$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle(2\gamma-d)^{+}$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2d$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$(2\gamma-d)^{+}$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2d$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$(2\gamma-d)^{+}$}}$}}.

(6.7)

The quantity
K_{\delta^{\prime},\gamma,\zeta^{\prime},d,q}
in (
6.3
) is nonpositive if

q{\left(\delta^{\prime}\gamma+\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\zeta^{\prime}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\zeta^{\prime}$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\zeta^{\prime}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\zeta^{\prime}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}+\gamma+\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}\right)}\leq d-1\iff q\leq q_{min}(d,\delta^{\prime},\gamma,\zeta^{\prime})\coloneq\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2d-2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2\delta^{\prime}\gamma+\zeta^{\prime}+d-1+2\gamma$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2d-2$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2\delta^{\prime}\gamma+\zeta^{\prime}+d-1+2\gamma$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2d-2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2\delta^{\prime}\gamma+\zeta^{\prime}+d-1+2\gamma$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2d-2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2\delta^{\prime}\gamma+\zeta^{\prime}+d-1+2\gamma$}}$}}.

In order for this to be possible, we need
q_{min}>1
. However, we see that

q_{min}<\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2d-2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle 2-p$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle 2-p$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$2-p$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$2-p$}}$}}(d-1)+2\gamma$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2d-2$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2-p$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2-p$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2-p$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2-p$}}$}}(d-1)+2\gamma$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2d-2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle 2-p$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle 2-p$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$2-p$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$2-p$}}$}}(d-1)+2\gamma$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2d-2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle 2-p$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle 2-p$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$2-p$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$2-p$}}$}}(d-1)+2\gamma$}}$}}<2-p\leq 1,

due to (
6.2
) and the facts that
\delta^{\prime}>0,p\geq 1
. Thus,
K_{\delta^{\prime},\gamma,\zeta^{\prime},d,q}>0
, and we can divide by it, rewriting (
6.3
) as:

s<s_{max}(\delta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime}d,q,\gamma)\coloneq\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2\zeta^{\prime}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2\delta^{\prime}\gamma+\zeta^{\prime}+d-1+2\gamma-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}(d-1)$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2\zeta^{\prime}$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2\delta^{\prime}\gamma+\zeta^{\prime}+d-1+2\gamma-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$q$}}$}}(d-1)$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2\zeta^{\prime}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2\delta^{\prime}\gamma+\zeta^{\prime}+d-1+2\gamma-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}(d-1)$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2\zeta^{\prime}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2\delta^{\prime}\gamma+\zeta^{\prime}+d-1+2\gamma-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}(d-1)$}}$}}.

To have
s_{max}>1
, we require that:

\zeta^{\prime}>2\delta^{\prime}\gamma+d-1+2\gamma-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$q$}}$}}(d-1)\iff qH_{\delta^{\prime},\gamma,d,\zeta^{\prime}}\coloneq q{\left(2\delta^{\prime}\gamma+d-1+2\gamma-\zeta^{\prime}\right)}<2d-2.

We remark that

2\gamma(1+\delta^{\prime})-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2p-2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2-p$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2p-2$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2-p$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2p-2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2-p$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2p-2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2-p$}}$}}(d-1)>H_{\delta^{\prime},\gamma,d,\zeta^{\prime}}>d-1+2\gamma-2(\delta^{\prime}-\beta^{\prime})-d-1=2(\gamma-\delta^{\prime}+\beta^{\prime}-1),

which unfortunately does not let us determine the sign of
H_{\delta^{\prime},\gamma,d,\zeta^{\prime}}
. If
H_{\delta^{\prime},\gamma,d,\zeta^{\prime}}>0
, we can divide by it, and thus rewrite
s_{max}>1
as:

q<\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2d-2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2\delta^{\prime}\gamma+d-1+2\gamma-\zeta^{\prime}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2d-2$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2\delta^{\prime}\gamma+d-1+2\gamma-\zeta^{\prime}$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2d-2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2\delta^{\prime}\gamma+d-1+2\gamma-\zeta^{\prime}$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2d-2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2\delta^{\prime}\gamma+d-1+2\gamma-\zeta^{\prime}$}}$}}.

Otherwise
s_{max}>1
for any choice of
q
, so we can write:

q<q_{max}(\delta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime},d,\gamma)\coloneq\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2d-2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle(2\delta^{\prime}\gamma+d-1+2\gamma-\zeta^{\prime})^{+}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2d-2$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle(2\delta^{\prime}\gamma+d-1+2\gamma-\zeta^{\prime})^{+}$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2d-2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$(2\delta^{\prime}\gamma+d-1+2\gamma-\zeta^{\prime})^{+}$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2d-2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$(2\delta^{\prime}\gamma+d-1+2\gamma-\zeta^{\prime})^{+}$}}$}},

where “<1/0” is taken to mean “arbitrary”. To have
q_{max}>1
as well, we will need either
2\delta^{\prime}\gamma+d+2\gamma<\zeta^{\prime}+1
, or:

d-1>2\gamma(\delta^{\prime}+1)-\zeta^{\prime}\iff\gamma<\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle d-1+\zeta^{\prime}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2(\delta^{\prime}+1)$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle d-1+\zeta^{\prime}$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2(\delta^{\prime}+1)$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d-1+\zeta^{\prime}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2(\delta^{\prime}+1)$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d-1+\zeta^{\prime}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2(\delta^{\prime}+1)$}}$}}\iff\zeta^{\prime}>2\gamma(\delta^{\prime}+1)+1-d.

Compatibility of
q_{max}>1
with (
6.4
) means:

2\delta^{\prime}+d+1-2\beta^{\prime}>2\gamma(\delta^{\prime}+1)+1-d\iff 2d>2\beta^{\prime}+2\delta^{\prime}(\gamma-1)+2\gamma.

Compatibility of
q_{max}>1
with
H_{\delta^{\prime},\gamma,d,\zeta^{\prime}}>0
requires:

2\gamma(\delta^{\prime}+1)+1-d<2\delta^{\prime}\gamma+d-1+2\gamma\iff 2d-2>0,

which is of course true.

If instead
H_{\delta^{\prime},\gamma,d,\zeta^{\prime}}<0
, we will need this relation to be compatible with (
6.4
), which translates to

2\delta^{\prime}\gamma+d-1+2\gamma<2(\delta^{\prime}-\beta^{\prime})+d+1\iff\gamma(\delta^{\prime}+1)<\delta^{\prime}-\beta^{\prime}+1.

With all of this, we split the system of relations into two cases:
K_{\delta^{\prime},\gamma,\zeta^{\prime},d,q},H_{\delta^{\prime},\gamma,d,\zeta^{\prime}}>0
and
K_{\delta^{\prime},\gamma,\zeta^{\prime},d,q}>0,H_{\delta^{\prime},\gamma,d,\zeta^{\prime}}<0
. The relation system for the first case reads:

\left\{\begin{array}[]{lr}\mu,\nu,\sigma\!\in\!\mathbb{N}\\
\beta^{\prime},\delta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime}>0\\
p\!\in\![1,2)\\
s\geq 1\\
q>1\\
\delta^{\prime}<2\beta^{\prime}&\text{\eqref{eq:A}}\\
\zeta^{\prime}>\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle p$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2-p$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle p$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2-p$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$p$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2-p$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$p$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2-p$}}$}}(d-1)&\text{\eqref{eq:B}}\\
s<s_{max}(\delta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime}d,q,\gamma)=\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2\zeta^{\prime}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2\delta^{\prime}\gamma+\zeta^{\prime}+d-1+2\gamma-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}(d-1)$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2\zeta^{\prime}$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2\delta^{\prime}\gamma+\zeta^{\prime}+d-1+2\gamma-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$q$}}$}}(d-1)$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2\zeta^{\prime}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2\delta^{\prime}\gamma+\zeta^{\prime}+d-1+2\gamma-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}(d-1)$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2\zeta^{\prime}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2\delta^{\prime}\gamma+\zeta^{\prime}+d-1+2\gamma-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}(d-1)$}}$}}&\text{\eqref{eq:C}}\\
q<q_{max}(\delta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime},d,\gamma)=\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2d-2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2\delta^{\prime}\gamma+d-1+2\gamma-\zeta^{\prime}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2d-2$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2\delta^{\prime}\gamma+d-1+2\gamma-\zeta^{\prime}$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2d-2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2\delta^{\prime}\gamma+d-1+2\gamma-\zeta^{\prime}$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2d-2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2\delta^{\prime}\gamma+d-1+2\gamma-\zeta^{\prime}$}}$}}&s_{max}>1\\
\zeta^{\prime}<2\delta^{\prime}\gamma+d-1+2&H_{\delta^{\prime},\gamma,d,\zeta^{\prime}}>0\\
2\gamma(\delta^{\prime}+1)+1-d<\zeta^{\prime}&q_{max}>1\\
\beta^{\prime}+\delta^{\prime}(\gamma-1)+\gamma<d&\text{\eqref{eq:comp1}}\\
\zeta^{\prime}<2(\delta^{\prime}-\beta^{\prime})+d+1&\text{\eqref{eq:D}}\\
2\theta\leq\gamma+1\\
\gamma\leq d\\
q\leq\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2d$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle(2\gamma-d)^{+}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2d$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle(2\gamma-d)^{+}$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2d$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$(2\gamma-d)^{+}$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2d$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$(2\gamma-d)^{+}$}}$}}\end{array}\right.

The relation system for the second one reads:

\left\{\begin{array}[]{lr}\mu,\nu,\sigma\!\in\!\mathbb{N}\\
\beta^{\prime},\delta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime}>0\\
p\!\in\![1,2)\\
s\geq 1\\
q>1\\
\delta^{\prime}<2\beta^{\prime}&\text{\eqref{eq:A}}\\
\zeta^{\prime}>\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle p$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2-p$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle p$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2-p$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$p$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2-p$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$p$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2-p$}}$}}(d-1)&\text{\eqref{eq:B}}\\
s<s_{max}(\delta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime}d,q,\gamma)=\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2\zeta^{\prime}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2\delta^{\prime}\gamma+\zeta^{\prime}+d-1+2\gamma-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}(d-1)$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2\zeta^{\prime}$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2\delta^{\prime}\gamma+\zeta^{\prime}+d-1+2\gamma-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$q$}}$}}(d-1)$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2\zeta^{\prime}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2\delta^{\prime}\gamma+\zeta^{\prime}+d-1+2\gamma-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}(d-1)$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2\zeta^{\prime}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2\delta^{\prime}\gamma+\zeta^{\prime}+d-1+2\gamma-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}(d-1)$}}$}}&\text{\eqref{eq:C}}\\
\zeta^{\prime}\geq 2\delta^{\prime}\gamma+d-1+2&H_{\delta^{\prime},\gamma,d,\zeta^{\prime}}\leq 0\\
\gamma(\delta^{\prime}+1)<\delta^{\prime}-\beta^{\prime}+1&\text{\eqref{eq:comp2}}\\
\zeta^{\prime}<2(\delta^{\prime}-\beta^{\prime})+d+1&\text{\eqref{eq:D}}\\
2\theta\leq\gamma+1\\
\gamma\leq d\\
q\leq\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2d$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle(2\gamma-d)^{+}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2d$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle(2\gamma-d)^{+}$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2d$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$(2\gamma-d)^{+}$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2d$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$(2\gamma-d)^{+}$}}$}}\end{array}\right.

Let us start from the second system, (
6.1
).
q
is arbitrary but larger than 1, since it has no upper bounds. As for
s
, we see that
s_{max}
is
\delta^{\prime}
-decreasing, so we would like to set
\delta^{\prime}=0
. With this choice, (
6.1
) becomes
\gamma<1-\beta^{\prime}
, which means
\gamma\leq d
and
q\leq\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2d$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle(2\gamma-d)^{+}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2d$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle(2\gamma-d)^{+}$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2d$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$(2\gamma-d)^{+}$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2d$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$(2\gamma-d)^{+}$}}$}}
are automatic. We thus rewrite the system:

\left\{\begin{array}[]{lr}\mu,\nu,\sigma\!\in\!\mathbb{N}\\
\beta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime}>0\\
p\!\in\![1,2)\\
s\geq 1\\
q>1\\
\zeta^{\prime}>\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle p$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2-p$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle p$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2-p$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$p$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2-p$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$p$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2-p$}}$}}(d-1)&\text{\eqref{eq:B}}\\
s<s_{max}^{\prime}(\zeta^{\prime}d,q,\gamma)\coloneq\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2\zeta^{\prime}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\zeta^{\prime}+d-1+2\gamma-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}(d-1)$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2\zeta^{\prime}$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\zeta^{\prime}+d-1+2\gamma-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$q$}}$}}(d-1)$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2\zeta^{\prime}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\zeta^{\prime}+d-1+2\gamma-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}(d-1)$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2\zeta^{\prime}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\zeta^{\prime}+d-1+2\gamma-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}(d-1)$}}$}}&\text{\eqref{eq:C}}\\
\zeta^{\prime}\geq 2\gamma+d-1&H_{\delta^{\prime},\gamma,d,\zeta^{\prime}}\leq 0\\
\gamma<1-\beta^{\prime}&\text{\eqref{eq:comp2}}\\
\zeta^{\prime}<d+1-2\beta^{\prime}&\text{\eqref{eq:D}}\\
2\theta\leq\gamma+1\\
\end{array}\right.

\beta^{\prime}=0
is the optimal choice for regularity range. We now test the compatibility of (
6.4
) and (
6.2
), which with
\beta^{\prime}=0
reduces to

d+1>\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle p$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2-p$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle p$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2-p$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$p$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2-p$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$p$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2-p$}}$}}(d-1)\iff(2-p)(d+1)>p(d-1)\iff 2pd<2d+2\iff p<1+\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle d$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle d$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$d$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$d$}}$}}.

Let us now compare (
6.2
) with
H<0
. The former will be the stricter bound if and only if

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle p$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2-p$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle p$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2-p$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$p$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2-p$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$p$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2-p$}}$}}(d-1)>d-1+2\gamma\iff{}

\displaystyle\gamma<\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle p-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2-p$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle p-1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2-p$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$p-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2-p$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$p-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2-p$}}$}}(d-1)

\displaystyle{}\iff{}

\displaystyle 2\gamma+d-1<p(\gamma+d-1)

\displaystyle{}\iff{}

\displaystyle p>p_{0}(\gamma,d)\coloneq 1+\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\gamma+d-1$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\gamma$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\gamma+d-1$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\gamma+d-1$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\gamma+d-1$}}$}}>1.

We now remark that, since
\gamma<1
,
p_{0}
satisfies
p<1+\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle d$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle d$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$d$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$d$}}$}}
if and only if

\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\gamma+d-1$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\gamma$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\gamma+d-1$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\gamma+d-1$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\gamma+d-1$}}$}}<\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle d$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle d$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$d$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$d$}}$}}\iff\gamma-2+d(1-\gamma)>0\iff d>d_{0}(\gamma)\coloneq\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1-\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2-\gamma$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1-\gamma$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2-\gamma$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1-\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2-\gamma$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1-\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2-\gamma$}}$}},

which is automatic since
d_{0}<\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}<2\leq d
. This means that we have two cases:
p<p_{0}
, where
H<0
is stricter than (
6.2
), and
p_{0}\leq p<1+\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle d$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle d$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$d$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$d$}}$}}
, where the opposite is true.

With all of this, we wonder if
s_{max}^{\prime}(\zeta^{\prime},d,q,\gamma)\coloneq s_{max}(0,\zeta^{\prime},d,q,\gamma)
is
\zeta^{\prime}
-decreasing. Let us compute the derivative:

\partial_{\zeta^{\prime}}s_{max}^{\prime}(\zeta^{\prime},d,q,\gamma)=\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2{\left(1-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}\right)}(d-1)+4\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle{\left[\zeta^{\prime}+2\gamma+{\left(1-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}\right)}(d-1)\right]}^{2}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2{\left(1-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$q$}}$}}\right)}(d-1)+4\gamma$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle{\left[\zeta^{\prime}+2\gamma+{\left(1-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$q$}}$}}\right)}(d-1)\right]}^{2}$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2{\left(1-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}\right)}(d-1)+4\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{${\left[\zeta^{\prime}+2\gamma+{\left(1-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}\right)}(d-1)\right]}^{2}$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2{\left(1-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}\right)}(d-1)+4\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{${\left[\zeta^{\prime}+2\gamma+{\left(1-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}\right)}(d-1)\right]}^{2}$}}$}}.

If
q>2
, we can guarantee that this is positive, meaning that
s_{max}^{\prime}
is
\zeta^{\prime}
-increasing, and thus is bounded by the value at
d+1
. Assume instead that the numerator is negative. Then
s_{max}
can do one of two things:

•

Reach infinity before
\zeta^{\prime}
reaches its lower bound
\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle p$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2-p$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle p$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2-p$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$p$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2-p$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$p$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2-p$}}$}}(d-1)
, in which case the upper bound on
s_{max}
will be
s_{max}<\infty
for
\zeta^{\prime}
somewhere in the range;

•

Not do that, in which case
s_{max}
is capped by its value at the largest of
\zeta^{\prime}_{0}(p,d)\coloneq\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle p$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2-p$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle p$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2-p$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$p$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2-p$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$p$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2-p$}}$}}(d-1)
and
\zeta^{\prime}_{1}(\gamma,d)\coloneq 2\gamma+d-1
.

In other words, one of the below will hold:

\displaystyle s_{max}^{\prime}\leq{}

\displaystyle s_{max}^{\prime}(\max\{\zeta^{\prime}_{0},\zeta^{\prime}_{1}\},d,q,\gamma)

\displaystyle s_{max}^{\prime}<{}

\displaystyle\infty\quad\wedge\quad s_{max}^{\prime}(\max\{\zeta^{\prime}_{0},\zeta^{\prime}_{1}\},d,q,\gamma)<0.

We now investigate the possibility of option 2.

\displaystyle s_{max}^{\prime}(\zeta^{\prime}_{0},d,q,\gamma)={}

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle p$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle 2-p$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle \displaystyle{}={}

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle p(d-1)$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle{\left[1-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}(2-p)\right]}(d-1)+(2-p)\gamma$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle p(d-1)$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle{\left[1-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$q$}}$}}(2-p)\right]}(d-1)+(2-p)\gamma$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$p(d-1)$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{${\left[1-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}(2-p)\right]}(d-1)+(2-p)\gamma$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$p(d-1)$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{${\left[1-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}(2-p)\right]}(d-1)+(2-p)\gamma$}}$}}.

Set

f(p,d,\gamma,q)\coloneq d-1+(2-p){\left(\gamma-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$q$}}$}}\right)}.

f
is clearly monotonic in
p
, whether increasing or decreasing. We can thus bound it as follows:

\displaystyle f(p,d,\gamma,q)\geq{}

\displaystyle\min{\left\{f(1,d,\gamma,q),f{\left(1+\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle d$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle d$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$d$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$d$}}$}},d,\gamma,q\right)}\right\}}

\displaystyle{}={}

\displaystyle\min{\left\{d-1+\gamma-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$q$}}$}},d-1+{\left(1-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle d$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle d$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$d$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$d$}}$}}\right)}{\left(\gamma-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$q$}}$}}\right)}\right\}}

\displaystyle{}={}

\displaystyle\min{\left\{(d-1){\left(1-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$q$}}$}}\right)}+\gamma,(d-1){\left(1-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle qd$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle qd$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$qd$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$qd$}}$}}\right)}+{\left(1-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle d$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle d$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$d$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$d$}}$}}\right)}\gamma\right\}}>0,

since
d-1<d<qd,q>1
. So
f\leq 0
is incompatible with the system. This implies that, if
s_{max}
is negative at
\zeta^{\prime}_{1}
, then
\zeta^{\prime}_{1}<\zeta^{\prime}_{0}
, so the value of
s_{max}
at
\zeta^{\prime}_{1}
does not matter to us. Therefore, we have the following bound on
s
:

\displaystyle s<{}

\displaystyle s_{maxmax}(d,q,\gamma,p)

\displaystyle{}\coloneq{}

\displaystyle\max{\left\{s_{max}{\left(\zeta^{\prime}_{0},d,q,\gamma\right)}\,\,,\,\,s_{max}(d+1,d,q,\gamma),s_{max}(2\gamma+d-1,d,q,\gamma)\right\}}

\displaystyle{}={}

\displaystyle\max{\left\{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle p(d-1)$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle f(p,d,\gamma,q)$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle p(d-1)$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle f(p,d,\gamma,q)$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$p(d-1)$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$f(p,d,\gamma,q)$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$p(d-1)$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$f(p,d,\gamma,q)$}}$}}\,\,,\,\,\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle d+1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle d+\gamma-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle To write out the ranges of the various cases explicitly, let us see when
\partial_{\zeta^{\prime}}s^{\prime}_{max}>0
:

2{\left(1-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$q$}}$}}\right)}(d-1)+4\gamma>0\iff q(4\gamma+2d-2)>4(d-1)\iff q>\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2(d-1)$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2\gamma+d-1$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2(d-1)$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2\gamma+d-1$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2(d-1)$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2\gamma+d-1$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2(d-1)$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2\gamma+d-1$}}$}}.

The above discussion gives us three ranges:

\left\{\begin{array}[]{@{}l@{}}2\theta\leq\gamma+1\\
\gamma<1\\
p<1+\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle d$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle d$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$d$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$d$}}$}}\\
q>\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2d-2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2\gamma+d-1$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2d-2$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2\gamma+d-1$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2d-2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2\gamma+d-1$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2d-2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2\gamma+d-1$}}$}}\\
s<\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle d+1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle d+\gamma-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle d+1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle d+\gamma-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$q$}}$}}$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d+1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$d+\gamma-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d+1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$d+\gamma-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}$}}$}}\end{array}\right.\qquad\left\{\begin{array}[]{@{}l@{}}2\theta\leq\gamma+1\\
\gamma<1\\
p<1+\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\gamma+d-1$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\gamma$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\gamma+d-1$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\gamma+d-1$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\gamma+d-1$}}$}}\\
q\leq\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2d-2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2\gamma+d-1$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2d-2$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2\gamma+d-1$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2d-2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2\gamma+d-1$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2d-2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2\gamma+d-1$}}$}}\\
s<\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2\gamma+d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2\gamma+d-1-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2\gamma+d-1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2\gamma+d-1-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$q$}}$}}$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2\gamma+d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2\gamma+d-1-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2\gamma+d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2\gamma+d-1-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}$}}$}}\end{array}\right.\qquad\left\{\begin{array}[]{@{}l@{}}2\theta\leq\gamma+1\\
\gamma<1\\
p\!\ni\!{\left[1+\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\gamma+d-1$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\gamma$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\gamma+d-1$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\gamma+d-1$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\gamma+d-1$}}$}},1+\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle d$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle d$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$d$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$d$}}$}}\right)}\\
q\leq\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2d-2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2\gamma+d-1$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2d-2$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2\gamma+d-1$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2d-2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2\gamma+d-1$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2d-2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2\gamma+d-1$}}$}}\\
s<\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle p(d-1)$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle d-1+(2-p){\left(\gamma-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}\right)}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle p(d-1)$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle d-1+(2-p){\left(\gamma-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$q$}}$}}\right)}$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$p(d-1)$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$d-1+(2-p){\left(\gamma-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}\right)}$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$p(d-1)$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$d-1+(2-p){\left(\gamma-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}\right)}$}}$}}\end{array}\right..

Let us review the last system left to study, namely (
6.1
):

\left\{\begin{array}[]{lr}\mu,\nu,\sigma\!\in\!\mathbb{N}\\
\beta^{\prime},\delta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime}>0\\
p\!\in\![1,2)\\
s\geq 1\\
q>1\\
\delta^{\prime}<2\beta^{\prime}&\text{\eqref{eq:A}}\\
\zeta^{\prime}>\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle p$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2-p$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle p$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2-p$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$p$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2-p$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$p$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2-p$}}$}}(d-1)&\text{\eqref{eq:B}}\\
s<s_{max}(\delta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime}d,q,\gamma)=\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2\zeta^{\prime}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2\delta^{\prime}\gamma+\zeta^{\prime}+d-1+2\gamma-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}(d-1)$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2\zeta^{\prime}$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2\delta^{\prime}\gamma+\zeta^{\prime}+d-1+2\gamma-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$q$}}$}}(d-1)$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2\zeta^{\prime}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2\delta^{\prime}\gamma+\zeta^{\prime}+d-1+2\gamma-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}(d-1)$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2\zeta^{\prime}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2\delta^{\prime}\gamma+\zeta^{\prime}+d-1+2\gamma-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}(d-1)$}}$}}&\text{\eqref{eq:C}}\\
q<q_{max}(\delta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime},d,\gamma)=\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2d-2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2\delta^{\prime}\gamma+d-1+2\gamma-\zeta^{\prime}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2d-2$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2\delta^{\prime}\gamma+d-1+2\gamma-\zeta^{\prime}$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2d-2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2\delta^{\prime}\gamma+d-1+2\gamma-\zeta^{\prime}$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2d-2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2\delta^{\prime}\gamma+d-1+2\gamma-\zeta^{\prime}$}}$}}&s_{max}>1\\
\zeta^{\prime}<2\delta^{\prime}\gamma+d-1+2&H_{\delta^{\prime},\gamma,d,\zeta^{\prime}}>0\\
2\gamma(\delta^{\prime}+1)+1-d<\zeta^{\prime}&q_{max}>1\\
\beta^{\prime}+\delta^{\prime}(\gamma-1)+\gamma<d&\text{\eqref{eq:comp1}}\\
\zeta^{\prime}<2(\delta^{\prime}-\beta^{\prime})+d+1&\text{\eqref{eq:D}}\\
2\theta\leq\gamma+1\\
\gamma\leq d\\
q\leq\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2d$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle(2\gamma-d)^{+}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2d$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle(2\gamma-d)^{+}$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2d$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$(2\gamma-d)^{+}$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2d$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$(2\gamma-d)^{+}$}}$}}\end{array}\right.

The compatibility of
H>0
and (
6.2
) requires

2\delta^{\prime}\gamma+d-1+2\gamma>\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle p$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2-p$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle p$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2-p$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$p$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2-p$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$p$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2-p$}}$}}(d-1)\iff\gamma(1+\delta^{\prime})>\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle p-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2-p$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle p-1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2-p$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$p-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2-p$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$p-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2-p$}}$}}(d-1).

This is compatible with
\gamma\leq d
iff

d>\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle p-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2-p$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle p-1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2-p$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$p-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2-p$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$p-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2-p$}}$}}\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1+\delta^{\prime}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1+\delta^{\prime}$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$1+\delta^{\prime}$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$1+\delta^{\prime}$}}$}}\iff\delta^{\prime}d>\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle p-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2-p$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle p-1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2-p$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$p-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2-p$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$p-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2-p$}}$}}(d-1)-d=\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2pd-3d-p+1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2-p$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2pd-3d-p+1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2-p$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2pd-3d-p+1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2-p$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2pd-3d-p+1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2-p$}}$}}.

Since both
q_{max}
and
s_{max}
are
\delta^{\prime}
-decreasing, we would like to take
\delta^{\prime}=0
, which combined with this condition would mean:

2pd-3d-p+1\leq 0\iff d(2p-3)\leq p-1.

Here we either assume
p\leq\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 3$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 3$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$3$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$3$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}
, in which case this is obvious, or this is not true. So these compatibilities reduce to
p\leq\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 3$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 3$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$3$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$3$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}
. Once we take
\delta^{\prime}=0
, we will also want to take
\beta^{\prime}=0
, so as to maximize the ranges of both
\zeta^{\prime}
and
\gamma
. This will lead us to
\zeta^{\prime}<d+1
, which combined with (
6.2
) leads to
(d+1)(2-p)>p(d-1)
, or
2d+2-p>2pd-p
, or
p<1+\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle d$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle d$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$d$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$d$}}$}}
, which implies
p<\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 3$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 3$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$3$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$3$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}
.

In fact,
p<1+\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle d$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle d$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$d$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$d$}}$}}
implies the compatibility above follows by assuming
\gamma\geq 1
. Under that assumption, we compare (
6.4
) and
H>0
, and see that
d+1<2\gamma+d-1\iff 2\gamma-2>0
, which holds. This means that the stricter upper bound is always (
6.4
), and we can thus neglect
H>0
.

With all of that, we rewrite the system:

\left\{\begin{array}[]{lr}\mu,\nu,\sigma\!\in\!\mathbb{N}\\
\zeta^{\prime}>0\\
p\!\in\!{\left[1,1+\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle d$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle d$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$d$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$d$}}$}}\right)}\\
s\geq 1\\
q>1\\
\zeta^{\prime}>\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle p$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2-p$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle p$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2-p$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$p$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2-p$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$p$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2-p$}}$}}(d-1)&\text{\eqref{eq:B}}\\
s<s_{max}(0,\zeta^{\prime}d,q,\gamma)=\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2\zeta^{\prime}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\zeta^{\prime}+d-1+2\gamma-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}(d-1)$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2\zeta^{\prime}$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\zeta^{\prime}+d-1+2\gamma-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$q$}}$}}(d-1)$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2\zeta^{\prime}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\zeta^{\prime}+d-1+2\gamma-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}(d-1)$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2\zeta^{\prime}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\zeta^{\prime}+d-1+2\gamma-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}(d-1)$}}$}}\eqcolon s_{max}^{\prime}(\zeta^{\prime},d,q,\gamma)&\text{\eqref{eq:C}}\\
q<q_{max}(0,\zeta^{\prime},d,\gamma)=\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2d-2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle d-1+2\gamma-\zeta^{\prime}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2d-2$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle d-1+2\gamma-\zeta^{\prime}$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2d-2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$d-1+2\gamma-\zeta^{\prime}$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2d-2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$d-1+2\gamma-\zeta^{\prime}$}}$}}\eqcolon q_{max}^{\prime}(\zeta^{\prime},d,\gamma)&s_{max}>1\\
2\gamma+1-d<\zeta^{\prime}&q_{max}>1\\
\zeta^{\prime}<d+1&\text{\eqref{eq:D}}\\
\gamma\!\in\![1,d)\\
2\theta\leq\gamma+1\\
q\leq\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2d$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle(2\gamma-d)^{+}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2d$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle(2\gamma-d)^{+}$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2d$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$(2\gamma-d)^{+}$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2d$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$(2\gamma-d)^{+}$}}$}}\end{array}\right.

Since
q_{max}^{\prime}
is clearly
\zeta^{\prime}
-increasing, the optimal choice for it would be to maximize
\zeta^{\prime}
, i.e. choose
\zeta^{\prime}=d+1
. Let us now investigate the monotonicity of
s_{max}^{\prime}
w.r.t.
\zeta^{\prime}
:

\displaystyle\partial_{\zeta^{\prime}}s_{max}^{\prime}(\zeta^{\prime},d,q,\gamma)={}

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2\zeta^{\prime}+2d-2+4\gamma-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle 4$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle 4$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$4$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$4$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}(d-1)-2\zeta^{\prime}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle{\left(\zeta^{\prime}+d-1+2\gamma-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}(d-1)\right)}^{2}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2\zeta^{\prime}+2d-2+4\gamma-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 4$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 4$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$4$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$4$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$q$}}$}}(d-1)-2\zeta^{\prime}$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle{\left(\zeta^{\prime}+d-1+2\gamma-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle
