1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$2$}}$}}-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}\right)}(d-1)$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{${\left(\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle\zeta^{\prime}+d-1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\zeta^{\prime}+d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\zeta^{\prime}+d-1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\zeta^{\prime}+d-1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$2$}}$}}+\gamma-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}(d-1)\right)}^{2}$}}$}}.

The condition for this to be non-positive is

\gamma+{\left(\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$q$}}$}}\right)}(d-1)\leq 0\iff q\leq\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2d-2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2\gamma+d-1$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2d-2$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2\gamma+d-1$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2d-2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2\gamma+d-1$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2d-2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2\gamma+d-1$}}$}}=2-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 4\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2\gamma+d-1$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 4\gamma$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2\gamma+d-1$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$4\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2\gamma+d-1$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$4\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2\gamma+d-1$}}$}}\eqcolon q_{0}(d,\gamma)=q_{max}^{\prime}(0,d,\gamma).

q_{0}<q_{max}
whenever
\zeta^{\prime}>0
, which is a given.
q_{0}\leq 2
whenever
\gamma\geq 0
, also a given.
q_{0}>1
iff
d-1>2\gamma
, or
\gamma<\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}
, which is allowed. So for large
\gamma
the system will not allow
q\leq q_{0}
, and thus
s_{max}^{\prime}
will be
\zeta^{\prime}
-increasing. For small enough
\gamma
, however, if
q
is large enough,
s_{max}
is
\zeta^{\prime}
-increasing, otherwise it is
\zeta^{\prime}
-decreasing. For
q=q_{0}
we have
s_{max}\equiv 2
.

In any case, the sign of this derivative is independent of
\zeta^{\prime}
, so the monotonicity is guaranteed. We have three bounds on
\zeta^{\prime}
: the two lower bounds (
6.2
) and
q_{max}>1
, and the one upper bound (
6.4
). Concerning (
6.2
) and
q_{max}>1
, let us investigate the relation between their bounding quantities.

\displaystyle 2\gamma+1-d>\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle p$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2-p$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle p$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2-p$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$p$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2-p$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$p$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2-p$}}$}}(d-1)\iff{}

\displaystyle\gamma>\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2-p$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2-p$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2-p$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2-p$}}$}}\iff 2-p>\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\gamma$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\gamma$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\gamma$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\gamma$}}$}}

\displaystyle{}\iff{}

\displaystyle p<2-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\gamma$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\gamma$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\gamma$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\gamma$}}$}}=\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2\gamma+1-d$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\gamma$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2\gamma+1-d$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\gamma$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2\gamma+1-d$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\gamma$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2\gamma+1-d$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\gamma$}}$}}.

This means that, in order for (
6.2
) to follow from
q_{max}>1
, we need this quantity to be greater than 1, which boils down to
\gamma>d-1
. This is not at all a guarantee, and is in fact close to saturating
\gamma<d
. We can therefore conclude the following:

\displaystyle s<{}

\displaystyle s_{maxmax}(d,q,p,\gamma)

\displaystyle{}\coloneq{}

\displaystyle\max{\left\{s_{max}^{\prime}{\left(\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle p$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2-p$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle p$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2-p$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$p$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2-p$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$p$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2-p$}}$}}(d-1),d,q,\gamma\right)},s_{max}^{\prime}{\left(2\gamma+1-d,d,q,\gamma\right)},s_{max}^{\prime}(d+1,d,q,\gamma)\right\}}.

But naturally the choice of
\zeta^{\prime}
has an impact on
q_{max}
, so we split this into three systems:

•

\zeta^{\prime}=d+1,q>q_{0}
;

•

\zeta^{\prime}=\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle p$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2-p$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle p$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2-p$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$p$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2-p$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$p$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2-p$}}$}}(d-1),q\leq q_{0},\gamma\leq d-1
;

•

\zeta^{\prime}=2\gamma+1-d,q\leq q_{0},\gamma>d-1
.

However,
q\leq q_{0}
requires
q_{0}>1
which implies
\gamma<\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}
, so we immediately discard the last system. The first system will therefore read:

\left\{\begin{array}[]{lr}\mu,\nu,\sigma\!\in\!\mathbb{N}\\
p\!\in\!{\left[1,1+\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle d$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle d$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$d$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$d$}}$}}\right)}\\
s\geq 1\\
q>q_{0}\\
s<s_{max}^{\prime}(d+1,d,q,\gamma)=\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle d+1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle d+\gamma-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}(d-1)$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle d+1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle d+\gamma-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$q$}}$}}(d-1)$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d+1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$d+\gamma-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}(d-1)$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d+1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$d+\gamma-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}(d-1)$}}$}}\eqcolon s_{top,1}(d,q,\gamma)\\
q<q_{max}^{\prime}(d+1,d,\gamma)=\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\gamma-1$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\gamma-1$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\gamma-1$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\gamma-1$}}$}}\eqcolon q_{top,1}(d,\gamma)\\
\gamma\!\in\![1,d)\\
2\theta\leq\gamma+1\\
q\leq\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2d$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle(2\gamma-d)^{+}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2d$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle(2\gamma-d)^{+}$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2d$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$(2\gamma-d)^{+}$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2d$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$(2\gamma-d)^{+}$}}$}}\end{array}\right.

For this to yield solutions, we must have
q_{top,2}>q_{0}
, i.e.:

\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\gamma-1$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\gamma-1$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\gamma-1$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\gamma-1$}}$}}>\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2d-2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2\gamma+d-1$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2d-2$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2\gamma+d-1$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2d-2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2\gamma+d-1$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2d-2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2\gamma+d-1$}}$}}\iff 2\gamma+d-1>2\gamma-2\iff d>-1,

which is true since
d\geq 2
.

We now compare the two upper bounds on
q
. The stricter one is
q\leq\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2d$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle(2\gamma-d)^{+}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2d$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle(2\gamma-d)^{+}$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2d$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$(2\gamma-d)^{+}$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2d$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$(2\gamma-d)^{+}$}}$}}
iff
\gamma>\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle d$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle d$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}
and

\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2d$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2\gamma-d$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2d$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2\gamma-d$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2d$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2\gamma-d$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2d$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2\gamma-d$}}$}}\leq\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\gamma-1$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\gamma-1$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\gamma-1$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\gamma-1$}}$}}\iff 2d(\gamma-1)\leq(d-1)(2\gamma-d)\iff 3d-d^{2}-2\gamma\geq 0.

Since
\gamma>\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle d$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle d$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}
, we see
3d-d^{2}-2\gamma<2d-d^{2}=d(2-d)<0
, so that the above never happens. In other words,
q\leq\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2d$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle(2\gamma-d)^{+}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2d$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle(2\gamma-d)^{+}$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2d$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$(2\gamma-d)^{+}$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2d$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$(2\gamma-d)^{+}$}}$}}
can be neglected. We thus obtain the following range:

\left\{\begin{array}[]{lr}\mu,\nu,\sigma\!\in\!\mathbb{N}\\
p\!\in\!{\left[1,1+\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle d$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle d$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$d$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$d$}}$}}\right)}\\[3.99994pt]
s\!\in\!{\left[1,\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle d+1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle d+\gamma-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}(d-1)$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle d+1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle d+\gamma-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$q$}}$}}(d-1)$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d+1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$d+\gamma-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}(d-1)$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d+1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$d+\gamma-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}(d-1)$}}$}}\right)}\\[8.00003pt]
q\!\in\!{\left(\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2d-2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2\gamma+d-1$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2d-2$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2\gamma+d-1$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2d-2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2\gamma+d-1$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2d-2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2\gamma+d-1$}}$}},\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\gamma-1$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\gamma-1$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\gamma-1$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\gamma-1$}}$}}\right)}\\
\gamma\!\in\![1,d)\\
2\theta\leq\gamma+1\\
\end{array}\right..

For the second case, we recall that
q\leq q_{0}<2
. This automatically guarantees
q\leq\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2d$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle(2\gamma-d)^{+}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2d$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle(2\gamma-d)^{+}$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2d$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$(2\gamma-d)^{+}$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2d$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$(2\gamma-d)^{+}$}}$}}
, since this is clearly
\gamma
-non-increasing and for
\gamma=d
it equals 2. Moreover, we already pointed out that
q_{0}<q_{max}
, so
q<q_{max}
is also automatic. One can actually see that
q_{max}>2
whenever
\gamma<\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}
. The system therefore reads:

\left\{\begin{array}[]{lr}\mu,\nu,\sigma\!\in\!\mathbb{N}\\
p\!\in\!{\left[1,1+\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle d$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle d$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$d$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$d$}}$}}\right)}\\
s\geq 1\\
1<q\leq q_{0}(d,\gamma)=\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2d-2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2\gamma+d-1$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2d-2$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2\gamma+d-1$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2d-2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2\gamma+d-1$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2d-2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2\gamma+d-1$}}$}}=2-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 4\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2\gamma+d-1$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 4\gamma$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2\gamma+d-1$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$4\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2\gamma+d-1$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$4\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2\gamma+d-1$}}$}}\\
s<s_{max}^{\prime}{\left(\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle p$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2-p$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle p$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2-p$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$p$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2-p$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$p$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2-p$}}$}}(d-1),d,q,\gamma\right)}=\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2p(d-1)$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle p(d-1)+(2-p)(d-1+2\gamma-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}(d-1)\!)$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2p(d-1)$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle p(d-1)+(2-p)(d-1+2\gamma-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$q$}}$}}(d-1)\!)$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2p(d-1)$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$p(d-1)+(2-p)(d-1+2\gamma-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}(d-1)\!)$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2p(d-1)$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$p(d-1)+(2-p)(d-1+2\gamma-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}(d-1)\!)$}}$}}\eqcolon s_{top,2}(p,d,q,\gamma)\\
\gamma<\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}\\
2\theta\leq\gamma+1\end{array}\right.

From the two expressions for
q_{0}
, we can see that it is
d
-increasing and
\gamma
-decreasing, as is expected.
s_{top,2}
is clearly
\gamma
-decreasing. Let us see that it is
d
-increasing:

\displaystyle\partial_{d}s_{top,2}(p,d,q,\gamma)={}

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2p{\left[2d-2+2(2-p){\left(\gamma-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}\right)}\right]}-2p(d-1){\left(2-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}(2-p)\right)}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle{\left[2d-2+2(2-p){\left(\gamma-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}})\right)}\right]}^{2}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2p{\left[2d-2+2(2-p){\left(\gamma-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$q$}}$}}\right)}\right]}-2p(d-1){\left(2-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$q$}}$}}(2-p)\right)}$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle{\left[2d-2+2(2-p){\left(\gamma-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$q$}}$}})\right)}\right]}^{2}$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2p{\left[2d-2+2(2-p){\left(\gamma-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}\right)}\right]}-2p(d-1){\left(2-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}(2-p)\right)}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{${\left[2d-2+2(2-p){\left(\gamma-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}})\right)}\right]}^{2}$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2p{\left[2d-2+2(2-p){\left(\gamma-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}})+\gamma(2-p)$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$(d-1)(1-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle q$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle q$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$q$}}$}})+\gamma(2-p)$}}$}}\right)}\\[8.00003pt]
q\!\in\!{\left(1,\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2d-2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2\gamma+d-1$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2d-2$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2\gamma+d-1$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2d-2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2\gamma+d-1$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2d-2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2\gamma+d-1$}}$}}\right]}\!\subseteq\!(1,2]\\[3.99994pt]
\gamma<\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle d-1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d-1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}\\
2\theta\leq\gamma+1\end{array}\right..

Appendix A
Improved Hölder inequality

In
[
3 (C-L)
, Appendix B]
, an improvement of the Hölder inequality is stated. Below, we state and prove a generalization of that result.

Lemma A.1
(name=Improved Hölder inequality,label=thm:lemma:ImprIHI).

Let
\lambda\!\in\!\mathbb{N}
and
f,g:\mathbb{T}^{d}\to\mathbb{R}
be
\mathcal{C}^{s}
function. Let
g_{\lambda}(x)\coloneq g(\lambda x)
. Then for every
p\!\in\![1,2],s\!\in\!(0,1)
:

{\left|{\left\|fg_{\lambda}\right\|}_{L^{p}}-{\left\|f\right\|}_{L^{p}}{\left\|g\right\|}_{L^{p}}\right|}\leq\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle C_{p}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\lambda^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle s$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle p^{\prime}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle p^{\prime}$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$s$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$p^{\prime}$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$s$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$p^{\prime}$}}$}}}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle C_{p}$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\lambda^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle s$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle p^{\prime}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle p^{\prime}$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$s$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$p^{\prime}$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$s$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$p^{\prime}$}}$}}}$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$C_{p}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\lambda^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle s$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle p^{\prime}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle p^{\prime}$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$s$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$p^{\prime}$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$s$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$p^{\prime}$}}$}}}$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$C_{p}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\lambda^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle s$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle p^{\prime}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle p^{\prime}$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$s$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$p^{\prime}$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$s$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$p^{\prime}$}}$}}}$}}$}}{\left\|f\right\|}_{\mathcal{C}^{s}}{\left\|g\right\|}_{L^{p}},

where all the norms are taken on
\mathbb{T}^{d}
and
p^{-1}+p^{\prime-1}=1
. In particular:

{\left\|fg_{\lambda}\right\|}_{L^{p}}\leq{\left\|f\right\|}_{L^{p}}{\left\|g\right\|}_{L^{p}}+\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle C_{p}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\lambda^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle s$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle p^{\prime}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle p^{\prime}$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$s$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$p^{\prime}$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$s$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$p^{\prime}$}}$}}}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle C_{p}$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\lambda^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle s$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle p^{\prime}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle p^{\prime}$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$s$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$p^{\prime}$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$s$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$p^{\prime}$}}$}}}$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$C_{p}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\lambda^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle s$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle p^{\prime}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle p^{\prime}$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$s$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$p^{\prime}$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$s$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$p^{\prime}$}}$}}}$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$C_{p}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\lambda^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle s$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle p^{\prime}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle p^{\prime}$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$s$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$p^{\prime}$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$s$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$p^{\prime}$}}$}}}$}}$}}{\left\|f\right\|}_{\mathcal{C}^{s}}{\left\|g\right\|}_{L^{p}}.

Proof.

Let us divide
\mathbb{T}^{d}
into
\lambda^{d}
small cubes
\{Q_{j}\}_{j}
of edge
\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\lambda$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\lambda$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\lambda$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\lambda$}}$}}
. On each
Q_{j}
we have:

\displaystyle\int\limits_{Q_{j}\mskip 4.0mu}^{\mskip 4.0mu}\hskip-2.0pt{\left|f(x)\right|}^{p}{\left|g_{\lambda}(x)\right|}^{p}\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mux={}

\displaystyle\int\limits_{Q_{j}\mskip 4.0mu}^{\mskip 4.0mu}\hskip-2.0pt{\left({\left|f(x)\right|}^{p}-\mathop{}\mkern-2.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.27777pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.27777pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.56596pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.27084pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-2.0mu\int_{Q_{j}}{\left|f(y)\right|}^{p}\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5muy\right)}{\left|g_{\lambda}(x)\right|}^{p}\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mux

\displaystyle{}+\mathop{}\mkern-2.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.27777pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.27777pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.56596pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.27084pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-2.0mu\int_{Q_{j}}{\left|f(y)\right|}^{p}\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5muy\int\limits_{Q_{j}\mskip 4.0mu}^{\mskip 4.0mu}\hskip-2.0pt{\left|g_{\lambda}(x)\right|}^{p}\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mux={}

\displaystyle{}\underset{\mathclap{\begin{subarray}{c}\Big|\\
\parbox{65.55579pt}{\centering\linespread\scriptsize$g_{\lambda}(x)=g(\lambda x)$ and change variables in the integral in term 2\@add@centering}\end{subarray}}}{=}{}

\displaystyle\int\limits_{Q_{j}\mskip 4.0mu}^{\mskip 4.0mu}\hskip-2.0pt{\left({\left|f(x)\right|}^{p}-\mathop{}\mkern-2.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.27777pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.27777pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.56596pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.27084pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-2.0mu\int_{Q_{j}}{\left|f(y)\right|}^{p}\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5muy\right)}{\left|g_{\lambda}(x)\right|}^{p}\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mux

\displaystyle{}+\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\lambda^{d}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\lambda^{d}$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\lambda^{d}$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\lambda^{d}$}}$}}\mathop{}\mkern-2.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.27777pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.27777pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.56596pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.27084pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-2.0mu\int{Q_{j}}{\left|f(y)\right|}^{p}\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5muy\int\limits_{\mathbb{T}^{d}\mskip 4.0mu}^{\mskip 4.0mu}\hskip-2.0pt{\left|g(x)\right|}^{p}\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mux={}

\displaystyle{}\underset{\mathclap{\begin{subarray}{c}\Big|\\
\parbox{65.55579pt}{\centering\linespread\scriptsize$|Q_{j}|=\lambda^{-d}$\@add@centering}\end{subarray}}}{=}{}

\displaystyle\int\limits_{Q_{j}\mskip 4.0mu}^{\mskip 4.0mu}\hskip-2.0pt{\left({\left|f(x)\right|}^{p}-\mathop{}\mkern-2.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.27777pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.27777pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.56596pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.27084pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-2.0mu\int{Q_{j}}{\left|f(y)\right|}^{p}\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5muy\right)}{\left|g_{\lambda}(x)\right|}^{p}\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mux

\displaystyle{}+\int\limits_{Q_{j}\mskip 4.0mu}^{\mskip 4.0mu}{\left|f(y)\right|}^{p}\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5muy\cdot\mskip-4.0mu\int\limits_{Q_{j}\mskip 4.0mu}^{\mskip 4.0mu}\hskip-2.0pt{\left|g(x)\right|}^{p}\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mux.

Summing over
j
we get:

{\left\|fg_{\lambda}\right\|}_{L^{p}(\mathbb{T}^{d})}^{p}={\left\|f\right\|}_{L^{p}}^{p}{\left\|g\right\|}_{L^{p}}^{p}+\sum_{j}\int\limits_{Q_{j}\mskip 4.0mu}^{\mskip 4.0mu}\hskip-2.0pt{\left({\left|f(x)\right|}^{p}-\mathop{}\mkern-2.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.27777pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.27777pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.56596pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.27084pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-2.0mu\int_{Q_{j}}{\left|f(y)\right|}^{p}\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5muy\right)}{\left|g_{\lambda}(x)\right|}^{p}\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mux.

Let us now estimate the second term in the RHS. For
x,y\!\in\!Q_{j}
, we have:

{\big|{\left|f(x)\right|}^{p}-{\left|f(y)\right|}^{p}\big|}\leq{\left|f(x)\right|}^{p-1}{\big|{\left|f(x)\right|}-{\left|f(y)\right|}\big|}+{\left|f(y\right|}{\big|{\left|f(x)\right|}^{p-1}-{\left|f(y)\right|}^{p-1}\big|}.

Since
p-1<1
, raising to the power
p-1
is a concave function, meaning
||f(x)|^{p-1}-|f(y)|^{p-1}|\lesssim|f(x)-f(y)|^{p-1}
. Therefore:

\displaystyle{\big|{\left|f(x)\right|}^{p}-{\left|f(y)\right|}^{p}\big|}\leq{}

\displaystyle{\left\|f\right\|}_{\mathcal{C}^{0}}^{p-1}{\left\|f\right\|}_{\mathcal{C}^{s}}\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\lambda^{s}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\lambda^{s}$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\lambda^{s}$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\lambda^{s}$}}$}}+K_{p}{\left\|f\right\|}_{\mathcal{C}^{0}}{\left\|f\right\|}_{\mathcal{C}^{s}}^{p-1}\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\lambda^{s(p-1)}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\lambda^{s(p-1)}$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\lambda^{s(p-1)}$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\lambda^{s(p-1)}$}}$}}

\displaystyle{}\leq{}

\displaystyle\lambda^{-s(p-1)}(K_{p}+\lambda^{-s(2-p)}){\left\|f\right\|}_{\mathcal{C}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}}^{p}.

Therefore:

\displaystyle\sum_{j}\int\limits_{Q_{j}\mskip 4.0mu}^{\mskip 4.0mu}\hskip-2.0pt{\left({\left|f(x)\right|}^{p}-\mathop{}\mkern-2.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.27777pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.27777pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.56596pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.27084pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-2.0mu\int_{Q_{j}}{\left|f(y)\right|}^{p}\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5muy\right)}{\left|g_{\lambda}(x)\right|}^{p}\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mux\leq{}

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle K^{\prime}_{p}{\left\|f\right\|}_{\mathcal{C}^{s}}^{p}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\lambda^{s(p-1)}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle K^{\prime}_{p}{\left\|f\right\|}_{\mathcal{C}^{s}}^{p}$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\lambda^{s(p-1)}$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$K^{\prime}_{p}{\left\|f\right\|}_{\mathcal{C}^{s}}^{p}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\lambda^{s(p-1)}$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$K^{\prime}_{p}{\left\|f\right\|}_{\mathcal{C}^{s}}^{p}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\lambda^{s(p-1)}$}}$}}\cdot\sum_{j}\int\limits_{Q_{j}\mskip 4.0mu}^{\mskip 4.0mu}\hskip-2.0pt{\left|g_{\lambda}(x)\right|}^{p}\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mux={}

\displaystyle{}={}

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle K^{\prime}_{p}{\left\|f\right\|}_{\mathcal{C}^{s}}^{p}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\lambda^{s(p-1)}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle K^{\prime}_{p}{\left\|f\right\|}_{\mathcal{C}^{s}}^{p}$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\lambda^{s(p-1)}$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$K^{\prime}_{p}{\left\|f\right\|}_{\mathcal{C}^{s}}^{p}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\lambda^{s(p-1)}$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$K^{\prime}_{p}{\left\|f\right\|}_{\mathcal{C}^{s}}^{p}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\lambda^{s(p-1)}$}}$}}{\left\|g_{\lambda}\right\|}_{L^{p}}^{p}={}

\displaystyle{}={}

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle K^{\prime}_{p}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\lambda^{s(p-1)}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle K^{\prime}_{p}$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\lambda^{s(p-1)}$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$K^{\prime}_{p}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\lambda^{s(p-1)}$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$K^{\prime}_{p}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\lambda^{s(p-1)}$}}$}}{\left\|f\right\|}_{\mathcal{C}^{s}}^{p}{\left\|g\right\|}_{L^{p}}^{p}.

This yields:

{\left|{\left\|fg_{\lambda}\right\|}_{L^{p}}-{\left\|f\right\|}_{L^{p}}{\left\|g\right\|}_{L^{p}}\right|}^{p}\lesssim_{p}{\left|{\left\|fg_{\lambda}\right\|}_{L^{p}}^{p}-{\left\|f\right\|}_{L^{p}}^{p}{\left\|g\right\|}_{L^{p}}^{p}\right|}\leq\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle K_{p}^{\prime p}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\lambda^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle sp$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle p^{\prime}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle sp$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle p^{\prime}$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$sp$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$p^{\prime}$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$sp$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$p^{\prime}$}}$}}}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle K_{p}^{\prime p}$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\lambda^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle sp$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle p^{\prime}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle sp$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle p^{\prime}$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$sp$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$p^{\prime}$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$sp$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$p^{\prime}$}}$}}}$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$K_{p}^{\prime p}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\lambda^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle sp$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle p^{\prime}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle sp$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle p^{\prime}$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$sp$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$p^{\prime}$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$sp$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$p^{\prime}$}}$}}}$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$K_{p}^{\prime p}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\lambda^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle sp$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle p^{\prime}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle sp$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle p^{\prime}$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$sp$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$p^{\prime}$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$sp$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$p^{\prime}$}}$}}}$}}$}}{\left\|f\right\|}_{\mathcal{C}^{s}}^{p}{\left\|g\right\|}_{L^{p}}^{p},

where in the first step we used that
p>1
, thus the power
p
is a convex function. We then take
p
th roots, and obtain the first estimate, where
C_{p}=K^{\prime}_{p}\cdot H_{p}
,
H_{p}
being the implicit constant in the first step. The second one follows trivially from the first.
\Diamondx

Remark A.1
(name=Greater exponents,label=thm:oss:GreaterExp).

For
p>2
, one gets
p-\lfloor p\rfloor
instead of
p-1
, and has to insert
\lfloor p\rfloor
intermediate terms when estimating
||f(x)|^{p}-|f(y)|^{p}|
.

Appendix B
Antidivergences

This appendix is devoted to the results on antidivergence operators found in
[
3 (C-L)
, Appendix B]
.

For any
f\!\in\!\mathcal{C}^{\infty}(\mathbb{T}^{d})
, there exists
v\!\in\!\mathcal{C}^{\infty}_{0}(\mathbb{T}^{d})
such that:

\Deltaup v=f-\mathop{}\mkern-2.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.27777pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.27777pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.56596pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.27084pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-2.0mu\int_{\mathbb{T}^{d}}f.

We denote
v
by
\Deltaup^{-1}f
. Note that if
f\!\in\!\mathcal{C}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathbb{T}^{d})
, then by rescaling we have:

\Deltaup^{-1}(f(\sigma\cdot)\!)=\sigma^{-2}v(\sigma\cdot)\qquad\sigma\!\in\!\mathbb{N}.

We recall the following antidivergence operator
\mathcal{R}
.

Definition B.1
(name=Tensor antidivergence,label=thm:defi:TensAntidiv).

We define the operator
\mathcal{R}:\mathcal{C}^{\infty}(\mathbb{T}^{d},\mathbb{R}^{d})\to\mathcal{C}^{\infty}(\mathbb{T}^{d},\mathcal{S}_{0}^{d\times d})
as:

(\mathcal{R}v)_{ij}=\mathcal{R}_{ijk}v_{k},

where:

\mathcal{R}_{ijk}=\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle d-2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle d-1$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle d-2$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle d-1$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d-2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$d-1$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d-2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$d-1$}}$}}\Deltaup^{-2}\partial_{i}\partial_{j}\partial_{k}-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle d-1$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle d-1$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$d-1$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$d-1$}}$}}\Deltaup^{-1}\partial_{k}\delta_{ij}+\Deltaup^{-1}\partial_{i}\delta_{jk}+\Deltaup^{-1}\partial_{j}\delta_{ik}.

It is clear that
\mathcal{R}
is well defined since
\mathcal{R}_{ijk}
is symmetric in
i,j
and taking trace gives:

\displaystyle\operatorname{Tr}\mathcal{R}v={}

\displaystyle-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle d-2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle d-1$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle d-2$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle d-1$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d-2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$d-1$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d-2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$d-1$}}$}}\Deltaup^{-1}\partial_{k}v_{k}-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle d$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle d-1$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle d$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle d-1$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$d-1$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$d-1$}}$}}\Deltaup^{-1}\partial_{k}v_{k}+\Deltaup^{-1}\partial_{k}v_{k}+\Deltaup^{-1}\partial_{k}v_{k}={}

\displaystyle{}={}

\displaystyle{\left(\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2-d$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle d-1$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2-d$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle d-1$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2-d$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$d-1$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2-d$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$d-1$}}$}}-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle d$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle d-1$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle d$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle d-1$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$d-1$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$d$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$d-1$}}$}}+2\right)}\Deltaup^{-1}\partial_{k}v_{k}=0.

By a direct computation, one can also show that:

\displaystyle\operatorname{div}(\mathcal{R}v)={}

\displaystyle v-\mathop{}\mkern-2.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.27777pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.27777pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.56596pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.27084pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-2.0mu\int_{\mathbb{T}^{d}}v\qquad\forall v\!\in\!\mathcal{C}^{\infty}(\mathbb{T}^{d},\mathbb{R}^{d})

\displaystyle\operatorname{div}(\mathcal{R}\Deltaup v)={}

\displaystyle\nabla v+\nabla^{T}v\qquad\forall v\!\in\!\mathcal{C}^{\infty}(\mathbb{T}^{d},\mathbb{R}^{d}):\operatorname{div}v=0.

(B.3)

We can show that
\mathcal{R}
is bounded in
L^{p}(\mathbb{T}^{d})
for
1\leq p\leq\infty
.

Theorem B.1
(name=Lebesgue-space boundedness of the antidivergence,label=thm:teor:LpBoundR).

Let
1\leq p\leq\infty
. For any
f\!\in\!\mathcal{C}^{\infty}_{0}(\mathbb{T}^{d})
there holds:

{\left\|\mathcal{R}f\right\|}_{L^{p}(\mathbb{T}^{d})}\lesssim{\left\|f\right\|}_{\mathbb{T}^{d}}.

The proof is left to the paper.

We can also introduce the bilinear version
\mathcal{B}:\mathcal{C}^{\infty}(\mathbb{T}^{d},\mathbb{R}^{d})\times\mathcal{C}^{\infty}(\mathbb{T}^{d},\mathcal{S}_{0}^{d\times d}\to\mathcal{C}^{\infty}_{0}(\mathbb{T}^{d},\mathcal{D}_{0}^{d\times d})
of
\mathcal{R}
. Let:

(\mathcal{B}(v,A)\!)_{ij}\coloneq v_{\ell}\mathcal{R}_{ijk}A_{\ell k}-\mathcal{R}(\partial_{i}v_{\ell}\mathcal{R}_{ijk}A_{\ell k}),

or by a slight abuse of notations:

\mathcal{B}(v,A)=\mathcal{R}Av-\mathcal{R}(\nabla v\mathcal{R}A).

Theorem B.2
(name=Bilinear antidivergence equation,label=thm:teor:BilinAntidivEq).

Let
1\leq p\leq\infty
. For any
v\!\in\!\mathcal{C}^{\infty}(\mathbb{T}^{d}),A\!\in\!\mathcal{C}^{\infty}_{0}(\mathbb{T}^{d},\mathbb{R}^{d})
:

\displaystyle\operatorname{div}(\mathcal{B}(v,A)\!)={}

\displaystyle vA-\mathop{}\mkern-2.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.27777pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.27777pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.56596pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.27084pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-2.0mu\int_{\mathbb{T}^{d}}vA

(B.4)

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathcal{B}(v,A)\right\|}_{L^{p}(\mathbb{T}^{d})}\lesssim{}

\displaystyle{\left\|v\right\|}_{\mathcal{C}^{1}(\mathbb{T}^{d})}{\left\|A\right\|}_{L^{p}}(\mathbb{T}^{d}).

Proof.

A direct computation gives:

\displaystyle\operatorname{div}(\mathcal{B}(v,A)\!)={}

\displaystyle\partial_{j}v_{\ell}\mathcal{R}_{ijk}A_{\ell k}+v_{\ell}\partial_{j}\mathcal{R}_{ijk}A_{\ell k}-\operatorname{div}\mathcal{R}(\partial_{i}v_{\ell}\mathcal{R}_{ijk}A_{\ell k}={}

\displaystyle{}={}

\displaystyle v_{\ell}A_{i\ell}+\mathop{}\mkern-2.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.27777pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.27777pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.56596pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.27084pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-2.0mu\int\partial_{i}v_{\ell}\mathcal{R}_{ijk}A_{\ell k},

where we have used the fact that
A
has zero mean and
\mathcal{R}
is symmetric. Integrating by parts, we have:

\mathop{}\mkern-2.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.27777pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.27777pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.56596pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.27084pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-2.0mu\int\partial_{i}v_{\ell}\mathcal{R}_{ijk}A_{\ell k}=-\mathop{}\mkern-2.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.27777pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.27777pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.56596pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.27084pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-2.0mu\int v_{\ell}\partial_{i}\mathcal{R}_{ijk}A_{\ell k}=-\mathop{}\mkern-2.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.27777pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.27777pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.56596pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.27084pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-2.0mu\int v_{\ell}A_{\ell j},

which implies that:

\operatorname{div}(\mathcal{B}(v,A)\!)=vA-\mathop{}\mkern-2.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.27777pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.27777pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.56596pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.27084pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-2.0mu\int vA.

Proving
(??)
involved proving the
\mathcal{R}_{ijk}
are bounded in
L^{p}
, so that:

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathcal{B}(v,A)\right\|}_{L^{p}}\leq{}

\displaystyle{\left\|(v_{\ell}\mathcal{R}_{ijk}A_{\ell k})_{ij}\right\|}_{L^{p}}-{\left\|\mathcal{R}(\partial_{i}v_{\ell}\mathcal{R}_{ijk}A_{\ell k})\right\|}_{L^{p}}\lesssim{\left\|v\right\|}_{L^{p}}{\left\|A\right\|}_{L^{p}}+{\left\|\nabla v\right\|}_{L^{p}}{\left\|A\right\|}_{L^{p}}\lesssim{}

\displaystyle{}\lesssim{}

\displaystyle{\left\|v\right\|}_{\mathcal{C}^{1}}{\left\|A\right\|}_{L^{p}}.

The proof is thus complete.
\Diamondx
